Fonte: design/tripo/necromante-roteiro-tripo.md

Necromante: roteiro Tripo, do piloto ao nível 150 (copia-e-cola)

PILOTO APROVADO (criador, 19/11), vale antes do Canvas 002: a roupa do conceito ficou (chapéu de aba, casaco longo azul-noite com faixas claras, bandoleira de velas, lanterna funerária de haste acima da cabeça) e é o nível 1. Rosto e corpo refeitos: meio-defunto, inquietante, nada de galã (pele cinza-branca azulada, bochechas fundas, olheiras, olhos lavados, lábios rachados, rosto longo e ossudo, veias escuras, corpo alto e magro de ossos aparentes, mãos ossudas). Mulher igualmente pálida e sinistra, nunca bonita, cabelo preto em coque sob o chapéu, corte feminino real. Pose T com expressão neutra e boca fechada. Bloco de identidade reescrito nos 148 corpos (Forbidden: galã, astro, modelo, pele bronzeada, caveira, foice, cartoon, brilho só no 150); fundo único #7A7A7A de estúdio; corpo sempre ossudo, peças crescendo a cada nível. ITEM, ARMA, CRYSTAL TOUCH, SHOWPIECE e os Caídos não mudaram.
Resumo (5 linhas)
1. Classe Roupão (casacão de vigia), cor índigo-noite #26306A (ajustada em 19/11) + fuligem #1C1C22; a chama âmbar #F2A33A é o único ponto quente, pintada, sem emissão; cera #D8CDB0 só em detalhes. Arma: lanterna funerária de haste (o livro é do Oráculo, não entra).
2. Ordem: conceito piloto M S1 → pose T → corpo F → cabeças → mãos → cadeia 1-150 (74 designs: 8 únicos + 33 níveis × ramos A/B) → Caídos → 2 prompts fixos de item.
PADRÃO FINAL (aprovado pelo criador, 19/11): todo nível, chave ou intermediário, parte do CORPO BASE em pose T e o prompt diz "IGNORE his/her current clothes and dress him/her in a COMPLETELY NEW level N outfit", descrevendo a roupa inteira do zero (as intermediárias antigas "REPLACE/NEW" foram fundidas com o traje da chave). Níveis 1-37: HOMEM, MULHER e ARMA. Níveis 41-150: ITEM + HOMEM-A/B + MULHER-A/B + uma ARMA por nível. A ARMA parte da arma anterior e é REDESIGNED (cabeça e cabo novos). Conversão concluída.
3. Todo corpo (chave ou intermediário) parte do CORPO BASE em pose T e troca silhueta, botas, braçadeiras e peças de cima, visível a 10 m. Na ARMA, todo nível redesenha a cabeça E o cabo (mesma linha, item visivelmente outro).
4. Calibragem de poder: capa ou manto só a partir da S5 (nível 41+); antes, no máximo gola pequena; S2 e S3 novas e sóbrias; ornamento só da S4 em diante; sem assimetria nem ombro à mostra forçados; nos altos, capas cobrindo tudo (estilo Diablo IV). Só o 150 tem brilho sutil.
5. Níveis 1-37: 3 blocos completos (HOMEM, MULHER, ARMA). Níveis 41-150: ITEM, HOMEM-A/B, MULHER-A/B e ARMA (uma). Nunca usar asset da Asset Store ou do Fab como entrada.
Fontes: design/tripo/barbaro-cadeia-armaduras-1-150.md (modelo), design/gdd/necromante.md (§3.2, §3.7, §3.9), design/gdd/class-tree-v2.md §1.13, design/art/visual-equipamento-por-classe.md, design/art/cores-classes-distancia-2026-11-19.md. Nada foi gerado.
Passada de personalidade 19/11 (régua nº 1: "hoje TUDO é visual"; nível herói Lost Ark / Diablo IV desde o 1):
1. Identidade nova em todo prompt: coluna alta com UM ponto de luz (lanterna no gancho acima da cabeça), bandoleira de velas creme, tabuletas com lacre vermelho-lacre, capelo de aba creme com rabo de fitas de nomes (peça de cabeça fixa).
2. Contraste: índigo + fuligem + creme de cera como terceiro tom claro em blocos + lacres vermelho-lacre pequenos; âmbar só na chama; nunca borrão monocromático. Forbidden ampliado (ceifador, rosto na sombra, manga de mago, livro, padre, roxo).
3. Piloto: 1a = UM conceito por nó (o criador varia pela quantidade); 1b pose T; corpo F com corte feminino real.
4. Níveis 1-60 reescritos: troféus crescem (velas 3→ coroa de velas; etiquetas; lacres; sinos), BULK nos intermediários (26, 37, 44-60) e no 31, cada intermediário com ≥2 marcas novas de longe, 60 com SHOWPIECE + acentos vivos (ouro velho fosco, vermelho-lacre, creme), nunca escuro.
5. Do 61 ao 150 só o bloco de identidade foi trocado. Nada foi gerado.

Bloco de identidade (topo do roteiro)

Para o Necromante não virar mago, padre, ceifador nem cavaleiro, todo prompt de corpo (HOMEM e MULHER) já traz este bloco. Se escrever um prompt novo, colar o bloco igual (do 41 em diante, mais a cláusula CRYSTAL TOUCH no fim do prompt): `` NECROMANCER IDENTITY (keep always): an uncanny half-dead funeral lantern-bearer, the night watchman who calls the names of the dead, NOT a wizard, NOT a priest, NOT a grim reaper, NOT a knight, NOT a handsome hero. FACE AND BODY (keep always, never beautify): a half-dead, unsettling man, NOT handsome: very pale grey-white skin with a faint bluish tint, sunken cheeks, dark circles under the eyes, pale washed-out eyes, thin cracked lips, a long bony face with sharp cheekbones, thin black hair tied back at the nape, dark veins on the temples and neck, a tall thin body with visible bone structure, long bony hands, a sinister stillness; in the T-pose a neutral expression with the mouth closed. UNFORGETTABLE SILHOUETTE: a tall narrow gaunt column (long fitted watch coat, straight squared shoulders, tall boots) crowned by ONE point of light: a funeral lantern hanging from a crook on a tall haft held high above the head. SIGNATURE DETAILS always visible from far away: a diagonal bandolier of cream wax candles across the chest, frozen wax drips on the shoulders and edges, hanging name tags and small cream wax tablets sealed with small oxblood red wax seals. FIXED HEAD PIECE: the watchman's capelo, a stiff wide-brimmed night-blue hat with a cream band and a cream-trimmed brim, the face fully visible under the brim, a long tail of knotted name ribbons falling down the back from the hat band; it stays on at every level (any outfit hood is worn over its crown or rests on the shoulders, face always visible). COLOR CONTRAST: matte night indigo #26306A coat, soot black #1C1C22 belts, boots and gloves, cream wax #D8CDB0 as a clear light third tone in solid blocks (hood rim, lining, candles, tags, sash), small oxblood red seals, dull aged metal fittings, a painted amber flame #F2A33A as the only warm point; readable color blocks, never a monochrome dark blob. PROGRESSION: the body always stays tall, thin and bony (this is his identity), but the outfit pieces grow in volume and presence at every level, never lighter, smaller or plainer than the previous level. FORBIDDEN: handsome face, movie star, beautiful model, healthy tan skin, skulls, bare skeleton bones, green, scythe, cartoon, mask, veil, face hidden in shadow, pointed hat, wizard robe with wide bell sleeves, book or tome, priest stole or cross, purple or violet, plate armor, plate skirt, knightly look, shapeless robe, glowing parts (only at level 150, on the lantern flame and candle wicks). ` MULHER: o mesmo bloco, trocando FACE AND BODY e "his identity" por: ` FACE AND BODY (keep always, never beautify): a half-dead, unsettling woman, just as pale and sinister, NOT pretty, NOT a model: very pale grey-white skin with a faint bluish tint, sunken cheeks, dark circles under the eyes, pale washed-out eyes, thin cracked lips, a long bony face with sharp cheekbones, thin black hair pulled back into a tight bun under the hat with nothing falling on the shoulders, dark veins on the temples and neck, a tall thin body with visible bone structure in a real feminine cut, long bony hands, a sinister stillness; in the T-pose a neutral expression with the mouth closed. ... (this is her identity) ... `` Em traços desenháveis: coluna alta e estreita (casaco longo justo, ombros retos, botas altas) + um ponto de luz no alto (lanterna pendurada no gancho da haste, acima da cabeça). Detalhe-assinatura: bandoleira diagonal de velas creme, cera escorrida congelada nos ombros, etiquetas e tabuletas de cera com lacre vermelho-lacre. Peça de cabeça fixa: capelo de vigília, chapéu de aba duro azul-noite com faixa creme e rosto livre sob a aba, e um rabo longo nas costas com fitas de nomes (cresce a cada nível: sininhos no 31, berloque no 37). Contraste: índigo fosco + fuligem + creme como terceiro tom claro em blocos + lacres vermelhos pequenos; âmbar só na chama.

0. Como usar

Fluxo de cada bloco: REFERÊNCIA primeiro.

  1. HOMEM (nó "Gerar imagem"): entrada = sempre o CORPO BASE M em pose T (§1, 1b). Do 41 em diante há HOMEM-A e HOMEM-B (interpretações do item pelos ramos).
  2. MULHER: entrada = sempre o CORPO BASE F em pose T (§2) (MULHER-A e MULHER-B do 41 em diante).
  3. ARMA: da referência, "Extração de elementos" (só a lanterna) → "Gerar imagem" para endireitar. Vira o 3D da arma.
  4. ARMADURA: da HOMEM ou MULHER aprovada, template "Pose T" com a linha fixa abaixo → Multivista → 3D (só com o OK e o custo antes).

Linha fixa da ARMADURA (colar depois do template Pose T; troque [nível]): `` ARMADURA: Mesmo Necromante, mesmo rosto e corpo, mesma pose T, mãos vazias, sem arma, fundo cinza #7A7A7A liso. Same character and exact same [nível] outfit as the reference, lantern removed, front view, symmetric T-pose, palms down, neutral expression, mouth closed, matte, no glow. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. Multivista: front, back and side; the back shows the same pieces as the front. ``

Regras (já dentro dos prompts):

  • Paleta índigo #26306A + fuligem #1C1C22 + creme de cera #D8CDB0 como terceiro tom claro em blocos (aba do capelo, forro, velas, etiquetas, faixa) + lacres vermelho-lacre pequenos; material da série em 25-35%; âmbar só na chama e nas velas. Nunca borrão monocromático.
  • Silhueta "coluna com um ponto de luz no alto". Roupão: nunca placa nem saiote de placas; metal só em fivelas, castiçais, botas, braçadeiras e lanterna.
  • Sem metal no traje na S1 (a lanterna de lata é arma). Capelo de vigília (chapéu de aba com faixa creme, rosto livre, rabo de fitas de nomes) em todos os níveis; S4+ tocos de vela nos ombros.
  • Nunca crânio, osso à mostra, verde, foice, máscara, véu, chapéu pontudo. S11 usa cera e resina primeva no lugar do osso.
  • Nunca mais leve que a anterior. Frente e costas com as mesmas peças.
  • Corte feminino: cintura marcada, quadril, bota alta; pele à mostra só se couber naturalmente; cabelo preto em coque sob o chapéu, nada nos ombros.
  • Corpo sempre alto, magro e ossudo (identidade); as peças crescem em volume e presença a cada nível, nunca mais leves.
  • Fundo de todo corpo: cinza médio liso #7A7A7A, sem textura, pergaminho, chão nem vinheta; luz de estúdio frontal.
  • Ramos (do 40): A = hoste (estandarte, etiquetas, castiçais altos); B = guarda dos vivos (manto com capuz, lanterna enjaulada e baixa, muitas velas pequenas).

Prova antes de aprovar cada ARMADURA: miniatura em cinza ao lado da anterior e leitura a 10 m (e a 34 m contra Mago e Sombra; a lanterna fica sempre acima da cabeça).

1. Conceito do piloto masculino S1

1a. Conceito (UM por nó; o criador varia pela quantidade de imagens do nó, nunca "four concepts"): `` CONCEITO: Stylized fantasy RPG character, isometric action RPG hero quality like Lost Ark or Diablo IV, ONE single character, full body front view, a tall gaunt male funeral lantern-bearer at the start of his journey (level 1), simple but unforgettable. NECROMANCER IDENTITY (keep always): an uncanny half-dead funeral lantern-bearer, the night watchman who calls the names of the dead, NOT a wizard, NOT a priest, NOT a grim reaper, NOT a knight, NOT a handsome hero. FACE AND BODY (keep always, never beautify): a half-dead, unsettling man, NOT handsome: very pale grey-white skin with a faint bluish tint, sunken cheeks, dark circles under the eyes, pale washed-out eyes, thin cracked lips, a long bony face with sharp cheekbones, thin black hair tied back at the nape, dark veins on the temples and neck, a tall thin body with visible bone structure, long bony hands, a sinister stillness; in the T-pose a neutral expression with the mouth closed. UNFORGETTABLE SILHOUETTE: a tall narrow gaunt column (long fitted watch coat, straight squared shoulders, tall boots) crowned by ONE point of light: a funeral lantern hanging from a crook on a tall haft held high above the head. SIGNATURE DETAILS always visible from far away: a diagonal bandolier of cream wax candles across the chest, frozen wax drips on the shoulders and edges, hanging name tags and small cream wax tablets sealed with small oxblood red wax seals. FIXED HEAD PIECE: the watchman's capelo, a stiff wide-brimmed night-blue hat with a cream band and a cream-trimmed brim, the face fully visible under the brim, a long tail of knotted name ribbons falling down the back from the hat band; it stays on at every level (any outfit hood is worn over its crown or rests on the shoulders, face always visible). COLOR CONTRAST: matte night indigo #26306A coat, soot black #1C1C22 belts, boots and gloves, cream wax #D8CDB0 as a clear light third tone in solid blocks (hood rim, lining, candles, tags, sash), small oxblood red seals, dull aged metal fittings, a painted amber flame #F2A33A as the only warm point; readable color blocks, never a monochrome dark blob. PROGRESSION: the body always stays tall, thin and bony (this is his identity), but the outfit pieces grow in volume and presence at every level, never lighter, smaller or plainer than the previous level. FORBIDDEN: handsome face, movie star, beautiful model, healthy tan skin, skulls, bare skeleton bones, green, scythe, cartoon, mask, veil, face hidden in shadow, pointed hat, wizard robe with wide bell sleeves, book or tome, priest stole or cross, purple or violet, plate armor, plate skirt, knightly look, shapeless robe, glowing parts (only at level 150, on the lantern flame and candle wicks). OUTFIT: Level 1 starter outfit, simple but striking, no metal on the clothes: a long narrow dark night-blue indigo wool watch coat to the knees with clear cream bands along the front edges and the hem, a soot black rope belt, a diagonal leather bandolier across the chest with three cream wax candles in leather loops, one small cream wax name tablet with an oxblood red seal hanging at the hip, frozen wax drips on one shoulder, the capelo: a stiff wide-brimmed night-blue felt hat with a cream band, the face fully visible under the brim, and a long tail of two knotted name ribbons down the back, wrapped soot cloth cuffs, worn soot black leather boots to mid-calf. He holds a tall wooden pole high above his head with a small tin funeral lantern with amber glass hanging from a carved wooden crook. Hand-painted PBR look, saturated, readable silhouette, BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. Matte, no glow. ``

1b. Pose T do escolhido (CORPO BASE M; entrada de todos os corpos masculinos): `` POSE T: Same half-dead Necromancer as the input image, same pale gaunt face, same black hair tied back, same thin bony body and the same level 1 outfit: Level 1 starter outfit, simple but striking, no metal on the clothes: a long narrow dark night-blue indigo wool watch coat to the knees with clear cream bands along the front edges and the hem, a soot black rope belt, a diagonal leather bandolier across the chest with three cream wax candles in leather loops, one small cream wax name tablet with an oxblood red seal hanging at the hip, frozen wax drips on one shoulder, the capelo: a stiff wide-brimmed night-blue felt hat with a cream band, the face fully visible under the brim, and a long tail of two knotted name ribbons down the back, wrapped soot cloth cuffs, worn soot black leather boots to mid-calf. Symmetric T-pose, arms spread wide, palms down, empty hands, no weapon, no lantern, legs slightly apart, front view, neutral expression, mouth closed. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. Full body. Matte indigo #26306A and soot #1C1C22 with clear cream wax #D8CDB0 blocks, matte, no glow, no baked shadows. Multivista: front, back and side; the back shows the capelo tail with its name ribbons, the coat vent and the bandolier crossing. ``

2. Corpo feminino base (entrada de todos os corpos femininos)

`` CORPO F: Same Necromancer pilot as the input image in T-pose, now as a WOMAN with a real feminine design, not the man slimmed down: same level 1 outfit pieces (indigo watch coat with cream lining, rope belt, bandolier of three cream candles, wax name tablet with an oxblood seal, the brimmed capelo hat with its cream band and tail of name ribbons), same palette, same height. FEMININE CUT: a female face and figure, the coat re-tailored to her bust with a shaped bodice, the rope belt cinching a visibly narrow waist, the coat skirt flaring from her hips, long legs in tall worn boots above the knee. FACE AND BODY: a half-dead, unsettling woman, just as pale and sinister, NOT pretty, NOT a model: very pale grey-white skin with a faint bluish tint, sunken cheeks, dark circles under the eyes, pale washed-out eyes, thin cracked lips, a long bony face with sharp cheekbones, thin black hair pulled back into a tight bun under the hat with nothing falling on the shoulders, dark veins on the temples and neck, a tall thin body with visible bone structure in a real feminine cut, long bony hands, a sinister stillness; in the T-pose a neutral expression with the mouth closed. NOT pretty, NOT a model, NOT a slimmed man. Symmetric T-pose, arms spread wide, palms down, empty hands, no weapon, front view. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. Full body, matte, no glow. Keep her feminine cut. Multivista: front, back and side. ``

3. Cabeças

A peça fixa da classe, o capelo de vigília (chapéu de aba duro azul-noite com faixa creme, rosto livre sob a aba, rabo longo de fitas de nomes), fica em todas as cabeças. Todas as cabeças seguem o piloto: meio-defunto, pálido cinza-azulado, nunca galã nem bonita; homem com cabelo preso atrás, mulher com coque sob o chapéu.

3.1 Prompt da cabeça a partir de um corpo (corpo aprovado como entrada): `` CABEÇA: Head and neck of the same Necromancer as the input image, front view and three-quarter view, same capelo kept on the head (stiff wide-brimmed night-blue hat with a cream band, the face fully visible under the brim, a long tail of knotted name ribbons), stylized half-dead face, never handsome or pretty, very pale grey-white skin with a faint bluish tint, dark veins on the temples and neck, neutral expression, mouth closed, not photorealistic, clean neck cut so the head fits on the body. Hair and face as described: [DESCRIÇÃO]. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. Matte, no glow, no skull, no mask, no veil. ` 3.2 Três cabeças masculinas ` CABEÇA M1: [DESCRIÇÃO] = the approved pilot face: long bony face, sunken cheeks, dark circles, pale washed-out eyes, thin cracked lips, thin black hair tied back at the nape. ` ` CABEÇA M2: [DESCRIÇÃO] = very long hollow face, sharp jaw, deep-set pale grey eyes, thin black hair tied low at the nape with a wax cream cord, clean-shaven, a thin old scar across the lips. ` ` CABEÇA M3: [DESCRIÇÃO] = older gaunt face, deeply sunken temples, grey-streaked thin black hair tied back, sparse grey stubble, a wax-cream candle-drip mark on the left cheek, washed-out pale eyes. ` 3.3 Três cabeças femininas ` CABEÇA F1: [DESCRIÇÃO] = the approved pilot woman: long bony face, sunken cheeks, dark circles, pale washed-out eyes, thin cracked lips, thin black hair in a tight bun under the hat. ` ` CABEÇA F2: [DESCRIÇÃO] = narrow hollow face, sharp cheekbones, heavy dark circles, pale grey eyes, black hair braided into a tight low bun with a thin wax-cream ribbon, nothing on the shoulders. ` ` CABEÇA F3: [DESCRIÇÃO] = older severe gaunt face, deep-set washed-out eyes, grey-streaked black hair in a tight high bun under the hat, a faint wax-cream stripe on the lower lip. ``

4. Mãos

`` MÃO M: Right and left hands of the same Necromancer as the input image, male, bare, open palm and relaxed fist, long bony fingers, very pale grey-white skin with a faint bluish tint, visible knuckles and dark veins, a thin pale wax stain on the fingertips, a few old burn marks on the knuckles, clean wrist cut to fit a cuffed sleeve. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. Matte, no glow, no gloves. ` ` MÃO F: Right and left hands of the same Necromancer as the input image, female, bare, open palm and relaxed fist, long bony fingers, very pale grey-white skin with a faint bluish tint, visible knuckles and dark veins, a thin pale wax stain on the fingertips, a small old burn mark on one knuckle, clean wrist cut to fit a cuffed sleeve. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. Matte, no glow, no gloves. ``

5. Cadeia de armaduras do nível 1 ao 150

PADRÃO FINAL (aprovado pelo criador, 19/11).

  • Níveis 1 a 37: cada nível tem 3 blocos completos: HOMEM, MULHER e ARMA. O corpo parte sempre do CORPO BASE em pose T (HOMEM 1 / MULHER 1); o prompt manda ignorar a roupa atual e descreve a roupa inteira do zero (cada nível troca peças e materiais). A ARMA é REDESIGNED (cabeça e cabo novos) e parte da arma anterior.
  • Níveis 41 a 150 (ramos A e B): cada nível tem ITEM (a armadura sozinha, neutra, fundo carvão), HOMEM-A, HOMEM-B, MULHER-A, MULHER-B (o mesmo item interpretado pelo ramo A ou B da classe, ver design/gdd/class-tree-v2.md) e uma ARMA por nível (o desenho do ramo A serve de base neutra).
  • Contagem: 8 níveis até o 37 + 33 níveis de ramos = 41 blocos de nível, que somam 74 designs de corpo por sexo (8 + 33 x 2).
  • Os prompts fixos de arte do item e a Arte de apresentação (nível 50 e nível 150) estão no fim do arquivo.

Nível 1 · S1 chave · Lã e Lata (piloto)

Ideia: o vigia que acabou de começar; simples, mas já com as 4 marcas da classe (coluna, bandoleira de 3 velas, capelo de aba creme com rabo de fitas, tabuleta lacrada). Sem metal no traje.

Parte de: CORPO BASE em pose T (HOMEM 1 / MULHER 1); ARMA parte da arma anterior da cadeia, redesenhada.

HOMEM `` Same Necromancer as the input image, same face and body, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon. BACKGROUND: plain flat neutral mid-grey studio backdrop #7A7A7A, seamless and uniform, no texture, no parchment, no floor, no scenery, no vignette; soft even studio light from the front. IGNORE his current clothes and dress him in a COMPLETELY NEW level 1 outfit. NECROMANCER IDENTITY (keep always): an uncanny half-dead funeral lantern-bearer, the night watchman who calls the names of the dead, NOT a wizard, NOT a priest, NOT a grim reaper, NOT a knight, NOT a handsome hero. FACE AND BODY (keep always, never beautify): a half-dead, unsettling man, NOT handsome: very pale grey-white skin with a faint bluish tint, sunken cheeks, dark circles under the eyes, pale washed-out eyes, thin cracked lips, a long bony face with sharp cheekbones, thin black hair tied back at the nape, dark veins on the temples and neck, a tall thin body with visible bone structure, long bony hands, a sinister stillness; in the T-pose a neutral expression with the mouth closed. UNFORGETTABLE SILHOUETTE: a tall narrow gaunt column (long fitted watch coat, straight squared shoulders, tall boots) crowned by ONE point of light: a funeral lantern hanging from a crook on a tall haft held high above the head. SIGNATURE DETAILS always visible from far away: a diagonal bandolier of cream wax candles across the chest, frozen wax drips on the shoulders and edges, hanging name tags and small cream wax tablets sealed with small oxblood red wax seals. FIXED HEAD PIECE: the watchman's capelo, a stiff wide-brimmed night-blue hat with a cream band and a cream-trimmed brim, the face fully visible under the brim, a long tail of knotted name ribbons falling down the back from the hat band; it stays on at every level (any outfit hood is worn over its crown or rests on the shoulders, face always visible). COLOR CONTRAST: matte night indigo #26306A coat, soot black #1C1C22 belts, boots and gloves, cream wax #D8CDB0 as a clear light third tone in solid blocks (hood rim, lining, candles, tags, sash), small oxblood red seals, dull aged metal fittings, a painted amber flame #F2A33A as the only warm point; readable color blocks, never a monochrome dark blob. PROGRESSION: the body always stays tall, thin and bony (this is his identity), but the outfit pieces grow in volume and presence at every level, never lighter, smaller or plainer than the previous level. FORBIDDEN: handsome face, movie star, beautiful model, healthy tan skin, skulls, bare skeleton bones, green, scythe, cartoon, mask, veil, face hidden in shadow, pointed hat, wizard robe with wide bell sleeves, book or tome, priest stole or cross, purple or violet, plate armor, plate skirt, knightly look, shapeless robe, glowing parts (only at level 150, on the lantern flame and candle wicks). OUTFIT: Level 1 starter outfit, simple but striking, no metal on the clothes: a long narrow dark night-blue indigo wool watch coat to the knees with clear cream bands along the front edges and the hem, a soot black rope belt, a diagonal leather bandolier across the chest with three cream wax candles in leather loops, one small cream wax name tablet with an oxblood red seal hanging at the hip, frozen wax drips on one shoulder, the capelo: a stiff wide-brimmed night-blue felt hat with a cream band, the face fully visible under the brim, and a long tail of two knotted name ribbons down the back, wrapped soot cloth cuffs, worn soot black leather boots to mid-calf. Matte indigo #26306A and soot #1C1C22 with clear cream wax #D8CDB0 blocks, painted amber flame only on the lantern and candles, no skulls or bare bones, no plate armor, matte, no glow, full body. ` MULHER ` Same Necromancer as the input image, same face and body, same T-pose, empty hands, no weapon. BULK.

FACE AND BODY (keep always, never beautify): a half-dead, unsettling man, NOT handsome: very pale grey-white skin with a faint bluish tint, sunken cheeks, dark circles under the eyes, pale washed-out eyes, thin cracked lips, a long bony face with sharp cheekbones, thin black hair tied back at the nape, dark veins on the temples and neck, a tall thin body with visible bone structure, long bony hands, a sinister stillness; in the T-pose a neutral expression with the mouth closed. UNFORGETTABLE SILHOUETTE: a tall narrow gaunt column (long fitted watch coat, straight squared shoulders, tall boots) crowned by ONE point of light: a funeral lantern hanging from a crook on a tall haft held high above the head. SIGNATURE DETAILS always visible from far away: a diagonal bandolier of cream wax candles across the chest, frozen wax drips on the shoulders and edges, hanging name tags and small cream wax tablets sealed with small oxblood red wax seals. FIXED HEAD PIECE: the watchman's capelo, a stiff wide-brimmed night-blue hat with a cream band and a cream-trimmed brim, the face fully visible under the brim, a long tail of knotted name ribbons falling down the back from the hat band; it stays on at every level (any outfit hood is worn over its crown or rests on the shoulders, face always visible). COLOR CONTRAST: matte night indigo #26306A coat, soot black #1C1C22 belts, boots and gloves, cream wax #D8CDB0 as a clear light third tone in solid blocks (hood rim, lining, candles, tags, sash), small oxblood red seals, dull aged metal fittings, a painted amber flame #F2A33A as the only warm point; readable color blocks, never a monochrome dark blob. PROGRESSION: the body always stays tall, thin and bony (this is his identity), but the outfit pieces grow in volume and presence at every level, never lighter, smaller or plainer than the previous level. FORBIDDEN: handsome face, movie star, beautiful model, healthy tan skin, skulls, bare skeleton bones, green, scythe, cartoon, mask, veil, face hidden in shadow, pointed hat, wizard robe with wide bell sleeves, book or tome, priest stole or cross, purple or violet, plate armor, plate skirt, knightly look, shapeless robe, glowing parts (only at level 150, on the lantern flame and candle wicks). OUTFIT: a hooded floor-length indigo cloak embroidered with small iron stars, large meteorite-iron clasps at the throat and chest, a belt of small candles in star-iron cups, star-iron shod boots, star-iron cage-bracers. Keep indigo #26306A + soot #1C1C22 dominant, painted amber flame only on lantern and candles, no skulls or bare bones, matte, no glow, full body. Nível 150 · S15 chave de prestígio · A Prestígio da Hoste / B Prestígio da Vela (único com brilho)

Ideia A: tudo mais pesado e rico; o único efeito do traje: brasa sutil e baixa nos pavios das velas e na chama da lanterna. Ideia B: tudo mais pesado e rico; o único efeito do traje: brasa sutil nos pavios da coroa de velas e da lanterna-sol. Parte de: CORPO BASE em pose T (HOMEM 1 / MULHER 1) para os quatro corpos; ITEM e ARMA: ARMA parte da arma 145, redesenhada.

OUTFIT: heavier and more ornate than anything before: a giant hooded primordial mantle cinched at the waist with a wide hem of wax and resin and gold-bronze filigree, a taller crown of candles around the hood (face visible), massive boots with gold-bronze bands, thick filigree bracers, belt of countless candles; only the wicks of the crown candles and the lantern flame carry a very subtle faint warm ember glow, low intensity. Keep indigo #26306A + soot #1C1C22 dominant, amber #F2A33A on flame and candles, no skulls or bare bones, everything matte except the faint ember, full body. CRYSTAL TOUCH (subtle): a few small discreet pale-blue crystal inlays with a faint soft inner glow, a quiet accent only, never dominant. FEMININE CUT (a real woman's cut, never a slimmed man): the outfit is tailored to her bust, a visibly narrow cinched waist, the coat skirt flaring from her hips, long legs in tall boots. HAIR: her thin black hair pulled back into a tight bun under the hat, nothing falling on her shoulders. Keep her feminine cut. ` ARMA ` Extract only the lantern staff, then: the same prestige tower staff alone, REDESIGNED for level 150, vertical, side view, full length, NEW head huge lantern in a gold-bronze filigree frame, NEW haft gold-bronze banded stone column, plain light grey background, neutral light, no hands, no glow. CRYSTAL TOUCH (subtle): a few small discreet pale-blue crystal inlays with a faint soft inner glow, a quiet accent only, never dominant. ``

6. Os Caídos (servos de cera e fuligem)

Não são cadáveres nem têm caveira: soldados de uma hoste antiga feitos de cera #D8CDB0 e fuligem #1C1C22, com uma chama âmbar pequena e pintada no peito. Uma malha só, material de cera; nunca a cor de um jogador. Entrada: só imagens nossas.

Conceito (4 variações livres): `` CONCEITO CAÍDO: Stylized fantasy RPG minion, full body front view, a ghostly soldier of an old host made of cream wax #D8CDB0 and soot black #1C1C22, a humanoid shaped like a worn wax candle statue with drips running down the limbs, simple rounded wax helmet with a smooth blank face (not a skull), plain soot cloth tabard, a tiny amber flame #F2A33A painted in the chest, short wax spear. No skulls, no bones, no green, no exposed flesh, matte, no glow. Hand-painted PBR look, readable silhouette, plain grey background, neutral light, full body. Four different variations. ` Evolução 1 · Caído (nível 1-39): ` CAÍDO 1: Same wax soldier as the input image, same smooth blank face and body. IGNORE its current look and build a COMPLETELY NEW basic form: plain rounded wax helmet, soot cloth tabard, bare wax arms with heavy drips, short wax spear, small amber flame painted in the chest, small and slim. T-pose, arms spread wide, palms down, empty hands, plain grey background, neutral light, full body, cream wax #D8CDB0 and soot #1C1C22, matte, no glow, no skulls or bones. ` Evolução 2 · Caído Lanceiro e Escudeiro (40-A, nível 40+): ` CAÍDO 2: Same wax soldier as the input image, same smooth blank face and body. 7. Prompts FIXOS de arte do item (fundo carvão), iguais em todos os roteiros

Valem para todo nível e todo ramo. Entrada: ARMADURA = a imagem aprovada do ITEM do nível (ou da armadura montada); ARMA = a imagem aprovada da ARMA do nível.

No boots, no bracers, no gloves, no arms, no body. 8. Arte de apresentação

Duas versões, nível 50 e nível 150. Entrada: os HOMEM e MULHER aprovados do nível (no nível 50, os do nível 48; ramo à escolha) e as ARMAS aprovadas; o RETRATO usa a imagem do personagem escolhido. Prompts em inglês, para colar direto.

Nível 50

KEY ART 50 `` Epic key art for the Necromancer class of a dark fantasy RPG: the two attached characters (the male and the female necromancer) together, same faces, bodies and outfits, holding their funeral lanterns on tall poles in powerful battle poses, a night watchman yard of wax candles and iron lantern posts with drifting soot smoke, a single amber point of light and no skulls or bones, dramatic rim light, painterly game cover style, high detail, wide 16:9 composition, no text. ` RETRATO 50 ` Portrait bust of the attached character: same face, hair and outfit. Three-quarter view, confident look, dramatic rim light, dark charcoal background, painterly game portrait style, high detail, square 1:1, no text. ``
